MHI2 Crossflash:
From Škoda to Volkswagen

Complete guide: MHI2_ER_SKG13_P4526 → MHI2_ER_VWG13_K4525

MHI2Škoda → VWVariant Conflict fixNaviBase

Parts used

  • Main unit: 565035020A
  • Display: 5G6919606

Before installing the unit, the protruding tab on the blue connector must be trimmed. The same must be done on the pink connector.

The pink connector connects to the grey connector.

Preparation before installation

Before starting the installation, I strongly recommend backing up all coding from the old unit. In my case, I did not do this and after the replacement the unit started throwing errors.

I then had to recode the unit to match what my car actually has and what it does not have. Therefore, before removing the old radio, save:

  • Long coding
  • Short coding
  • Adaptations
  • Installed FECs
  • Software version
  • Part number and hardware version

The two most problematic parts were:

  • Changing the unit software
  • Fully updating NaviBase

Software crossflash

For the crossflash, I used the following guide:

Between Brands | MIB2 WIKI – Mirror

I was converting from:

MHI2_ER_SKG13_P4526
↓
MHI2_ER_VWG13_K4525

I followed all the steps described in the MIB Wiki, but when installing the system, the following error appeared:

“Variant Conflict”

This error prevented the software from being installed correctly.

Problem: Variant Conflict

In my case, the culprit turned out to be the Update-hashes program. The standard metainfo2.txt file did not pass validation and the update ended with a variant error.

The solution was to manually modify the metainfo2.txt file. I was helped by a Telegram user:

@fvolanti

Additionally, @fvolanti provided me with a modified update file that changed the unit number to the one used in Volkswagen vehicles. Thanks to this, after the crossflash, the unit was recognised as a correct VW variant instead of a Škoda unit.

How it worked in practice

  • @fvolanti prepared a modified update file.
  • The file changed the unit number to the correct VW one.
  • At the same time, I used the modified metainfo2.txt.
  • Thanks to this, the update no longer threw the “Variant Conflict” error.
  • After the first update, the system was partially installed.
  • After the second update, everything was correctly installed as a VW variant.

metainfo2.txt file

Below are the most important settings from the modified metainfo2.txt file that allowed me to bypass the “Variant Conflict” error.

Update: MHI2_ER_VWG13_K4525
MUVersion: 1367
Vendor: HARMAN (HAD)

release = "MHI2_ER_VWG13_K4525"
MUVersion = "1367"
vendor = "HARMAN (HAD)"
region = "Europe"
region2 = "RoW"
skipMetaCRC = "true"
checkIfNotEqual = "true"
skipCheckRegion = "true"
skipCheckSignatureAndVariant = "true"
delMmeBackup = "true"

Key crossflash entries

skipMetaCRC = "true"
skipCheckRegion = "true"
skipCheckSignatureAndVariant = "true"

Supported trains

SupportedTrains = "MHI2_ER_VWG11_*,MHI2_US_VWG13_*,MHI2_*"

Thanks to the MHI2_* entry, the update can be accepted by a unit originating from a Škoda, even though the original file was prepared for a VW variant.

Download: The complete modified metainfo2.txt file is included at the bottom of this page.

How to use the file

  1. Download the original MHI2_ER_VWG13_K4525 update.
  2. Extract the archive to a USB drive.
  3. Locate the metainfo2.txt file in the update folder.
  4. Make a copy of the original file — never work without a backup.
  5. Replace the original metainfo2.txt with the modified file provided below.
  6. Insert the USB drive into the car.
  7. Start the update from the MHI2 service menu.
  8. After the first update, check whether the system was partially installed.
  9. If mixed Škoda/VW elements appear, perform a second update using the same USB drive.
  10. After the second update, the system should be fully converted to the VW variant.

Important: Do not edit the checksums of the .usf, .bin files or system images themselves. Only the update description file — metainfo2.txt — is modified.

Navigation problem

The second major problem was the full navigation update. I spent about 4.5 hours flashing and updating the navigation without success. The FEC codes did not work as they should.

Thanks to user DrDray, I received a link to a GitHub page describing the exact problem I had:

mibwiki-snapshot / navigation.md

The problem concerned installing FEC codes that did not work correctly after the crossflash.

Navigation solution

  1. Install or launch M.I.B.
  2. Use M.I.B to clear the NaviBase memory.
  3. Install Toolbox.
  4. In Toolbox, change the Brand to VW.
  5. Enable the SWDL option.
  6. Only then proceed with the actual navigation update.

Thanks to this, after many hours of struggling, I was finally able to get a fully functional navigation system.

Coding after crossflash

After successfully installing the software, be sure to check the unit coding. In my case, after the crossflash, errors appeared because the unit was configured for equipment that my car did not have.

Go through the coding and disable or correct functions that the car does not have, such as:

  • Camera-related functions
  • Driver assistance systems
  • Headlights
  • Air conditioning
  • Park Assist
  • DAB
  • Audio amplifier
  • Škoda-specific functions

After correct coding, the errors should disappear and the unit should work stably.

Acknowledgements

Special thanks for the help to:

  • @DrDray
  • @AntoineAvecUnF
  • @witek10000
  • @xsopy
  • @fvolanti
  • and my friend Łukasz Ko, who helped me with the coding today.

Important note: This is a crossflash procedure, not a regular update. Before starting, make a backup of all coding, FECs and unit settings. Do not interrupt the update or remove the USB drive while it is flashing.

Complete metainfo2.txt

Below is the complete modified metainfo2.txt file used for the MHI2 crossflash.

[common]
vendor = "ESO"
variant = "FMU-H-*-*-*"
variant2 = "FM2-*-*-*-*"
variant3 = "QC2-*-*-*-*"
variant4 = "FMQ-*-*-*-*"
region = "RoW"
MetafileChecksum = "37259e4758d7c843f316aaaa306cced5211049cf"

[common_Release_1]
variant = "FMU-H-*-*-*"
region = "RoW"
name = "MIB1 navigation database"
path = "./Mib1"

[common_Release_2]
variant = "FM2-*-*-*-*"
variant2 = "QC2-*-*-*-*"
variant3 = "FMQ-*-*-*-*"
region = "RoW"
name = "MIB2 navigation database"
path = "./Mib2"
